# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-09-10)

## Corpus Check
- 2306 files · ~8,303,309 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 41828 nodes · 105418 edges · 1908 communities (1004 shown, 904 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2549 edges (avg confidence: 0.67)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `7f137783`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
